Latest evidence: [4 October HOME entry footer and HUD delay](home-entry-delay-2026-10-04.md), runtime `5973bcd6`. Earlier sections are historical measurements, not current feature status.

## HOME Entry Footer And HUD Delay - 4 October 2026

Native HOME initialization staggers the post-boot chrome: on a three-frame
capture grid (wallpaper phase), footer SceneIn starts about four frames after
the boot fade's terminal pose and the HUD about four after the footer. The
browser started both at the first HOME update. Worker `1609d7b3`/`93f7ddf8`
integrate as `03dd2450`/`5973bcd6`: footer +3 and HUD +7 updates (fitted
adaptations). Full 1994 pass/0 fail, build/typecheck and review pass.
Desktop/mobile 28 and reduced 3 warm-boot pairs recaptured. Aligned on the
footer, native HUD N061..N063 land exactly on browser updates 29/32/35.
[Evidence](home-entry-delay-2026-10-04.md).

## Evidence meanings

- **Implemented**: the integration source/delivery contains the path.
- **Tested**: automated checks exercise the stated contract or resource.
- **Browser-inspected**: the live console was operated and viewed for the named
  scenario. It is not a pixel or timing comparison.
- **Native-compared**: Azahar's own native-resolution LCD capture was paired
  with the raw integrated production-browser LCD target at the same state,
  inputs and frame. A source fixture or source render is a separate bounded
  source tier and does not establish native/browser fidelity.
- **Incomplete**: a known adaptation, missing source, unsupported format or
  unverified route remains.

Historical cells below sometimes use “native-compared” for source fixtures or
source renders. Read their stated evidence literally; those cells do **not**
meet the raw Azahar-versus-production-browser definition above. The 25
September acceptance note at the end of this record governs future claims.

## Acceptance status

The scoped system is substantially implemented and provenance-backed. Final
acceptance is still open, and strict 1:1 fidelity is unproven for every area.
The gaps are:

- No whole-screen matched native comparison of pixels, motion, audio and input
  timing exists for any screen.
- Game Notes' initial Double mode and switch sequence are source-traced and
  integrated ([audit](native-notes-switch-source-audit.md)); its Switch clip
  animates, title descriptions/icons are delivered, and the
  [accepted HOME exit](native-notes-accepted-home-entry-audit.md) has a bounded
  live owner correction. The title/HUD controller and two switch sounds are
  not displayed/played; matched native comparison remains open.
- Camera's [settled grid](camera-grid-source-audit.md) is sourced, but its
  [native scroll behavior](camera-paging-source-audit.md) is not implemented;
  footer and upper viewfinder adaptations remain open.
- Settings' [Open Blocks value](settings-open-blocks-source-audit.md) is derived
  from unavailable SD filesystem state; blank is deliberate rather than a
  completed native state.
- The parental notice has not had a matched native LCD/timing comparison.
- [Language arrow motion](settings-language-motion-validation.md) uses the
  original clips at nominal browser timing; press/repeat/D-pad behavior is
  still untraced. The [Update entry](updater-entry-source-audit.md) is a static
  source-backed prompt with inert OK, not a real device update flow.
- The [amiibo opening UI](native-amiibo-opening.md) is delivered only as an
  internal read-only applet; there is no visible entrypoint or accepted native
  LCD comparison. The NNID unsigned-in body is missing.
- Sound playback is unproven without supplied songs.
- The hardware model is not visually accepted.

The [feature map](feature-map.md) assigns each gap. The closing check is one
versioned scenario matrix in the browser and the verified isolated Azahar profile. It
should record LCD pairs, the input sequence, animation/audio timestamps and
intentional portfolio differences.

An isolated OpenGL reference run subsequently reached native Other Settings
page 1 through a source-supported keyboard-to-touch mapping. The paired browser
and native captures reveal a missing upper status strip in the browser Settings
composition; see [the bounded comparison](native-settings-live-comparison-2026-09-24.md).
This is not a pixel-aligned acceptance. The isolated profile was restored and
the default profile timestamp did not change.

An Azahar launch on this machine unexpectedly wrote emulator runtime state to
the default macOS profile despite an isolated path supplied to the process.
No capture from that session is accepted as native comparison evidence. Do
not launch native sessions from the installed app or rely on a shell working
directory override. The repository and delivered website were not affected
by those emulator profile writes. A subsequent
[guarded copied-bundle launch](native-reference-profile-isolation.md) did
isolate its profile and opened the native Settings main page, but coordinate
input could not reliably reach subpages; it is a smoke check, not an accepted
native comparison.

The user's final phrase in the originating brief ended “and create a”. No extra
deliverable is inferred from that incomplete phrase.

## Later UI integration checkpoint

The active continuation checkout is on the writable home disk because the SSD
refused new worktree writes. A copied, isolated Azahar bundle/profile captured
the native Health entry and settled Sound entry without using the default
profile. These are reference captures, not permission to claim all screens 1:1.

- Health entry now leaves all three buttons unselected until directional input.
  Its lower source LCD improved from 15.30 to **1.46/255 RGB MAE** against the
  native capture; the article and animated upper LCD remain open.
- Camera photo view no longer has invisible previous/next touch regions. In the
  rebuilt browser, empty-side taps left a multi-photo image unchanged, physical
  Right advanced it, and Back returned to the gallery.
- Game Notes now paints the original lower ApltBoot cover over its list until
  source pass 21. The source-frame comparison changes entry poses and keeps the
  settled grid identical. Work → HOME → Notes → HOME passed in the browser.
- The native Sound empty entry showed a much richer screen than the former
  blank browser state. Its source title/status, birds, row, controls and paired
  Record vinyl are integrated. The combined lower source LCD is **10.80/255
  RGB MAE** from the captured native LCD, versus **75.54/255** before the
  vinyl; upper is **18.61/255**, versus **23.40/255**. The upper room CGFX,
  first-run Welcome sequence, bird motion and song playback remain unresolved.
- Settings → Sound now shows the source Cancel/OK footer. Its browser route and
  Cancel return were checked; options and OK remain inert for this portfolio.

The combined source renderer produced **57 pairs with zero diagnostics**.
The production build, typecheck and full test suite passed: **1,331 passed,
21 skipped, 0 failed** with test artifacts redirected to the home disk and
the sparse checkout's model fixture linked read-only to the preserved SSD
worktree. Browser warning/error logs were empty on the exercised paths.
`docs/feature-map.md` and the cited per-screen evidence retain the unverified
scenarios. Strict 1:1 acceptance and the requested PR remain open.

### Azahar renderer and title-path audit — 27 September 2026

The Sandisk Azahar 2126.1.2 process (executable SHA-256
`3dfdfbed147cfb420f224385e832191833d07b0951d4b86326ab193e2deb3b21`) uses the
primary `camera-guide-replay-20260926` working directory, config, log, caches
and system profile. The Settings title opened in this run came from the
byte-identical `.app` file in `camera-guide-touch-replay-20260927`; do not use
that run as a pristine single-path replay. Both copies contain the same
Settings title SHA-256 `79087e9f7f62c616f27167e2623119ffc8350a1fa1a949f28280df9c789cbac0`.

The primary config requests `graphics_api=1`, but its runtime log reports
`Unknown or unsupported graphics API 1` and initializes Vulkan. Azahar's
2126.1.2 source enum defines OpenGL as 1, while the Apple CMake build disables
OpenGL; therefore the effective renderer is Vulkan. The existing config was
left untouched because both isolated Azahar processes remained alive after a
normal quit request. No new native screenshot or comparison was made in this
audit. Before the next replay, close all processes using this profile, version
the config, set `graphics_api=2`, and confirm Vulkan initialization in the log.
The settled browser is still Other Settings page 1 at port 3006; the latest
matrix v94 remains 166 entries, all failing whole-scenario acceptance.
